Model before rewriting:

find a: set (maxSize 2) of int(1..2)
find b: set (maxSize 2) of int(1..2)

such that

(a subsetEq b)

--

a
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
a
new variables:
  find a#packed_1: int(0..3)
new constraints:
  and([sum([(a#packed_1 / 1) % 2,(a#packed_1 / 2) % 2;int(1..)]) >= 0,sum([(a#packed_1 / 1) % 2,(a#packed_1 / 2) % 2;int(1..)]) <= 2;int(1..)])

--

b
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
b
new variables:
  find b#packed_1: int(0..3)
new constraints:

--

a#packed_1
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
a#packed_1

  find a#packed_1#direct_2: bool
  find a#packed_1#direct_3: bool
  find a#packed_1#direct_4: bool
new constraints:
  sum([toInt(a#packed_1#direct_1),toInt(a#packed_1#direct_2),toInt(a#packed_1#direct_3),toInt(a#packed_1#direct_4);int(1..)]) <= 1
  or([a#packed_1#direct_1,a#packed_1#direct_2,a#packed_1#direct_3,a#packed_1#direct_4;int(1..)])

   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
b#packed_1
new variables:

  find b#packed_1#direct_2: bool
  find b#packed_1#direct_3: bool
  find b#packed_1#direct_4: bool

(a subsetEq b),
and([sum([(a#packed_1 / 1) % 2,(a#packed_1 / 2) % 2;int(1..)]) >= 0,sum([(a#packed_1 / 1) % 2,(a#packed_1 / 2) % 2;int(1..)]) <= 2;int(1..)]),
and([sum([(b#packed_1 / 1) % 2,(b#packed_1 / 2) % 2;int(1..)]) >= 0,sum([(b#packed_1 / 1) % 2,(b#packed_1 / 2) % 2;int(1..)]) <= 2;int(1..)]),
sum([toInt(a#packed_1#direct_1),toInt(a#packed_1#direct_2),toInt(a#packed_1#direct_3),toInt(a#packed_1#direct_4);int(1..)]) <= 1,
or([a#packed_1#direct_1,a#packed_1#direct_2,a#packed_1#direct_3,a#packed_1#direct_4;int(1..)]),

or([b#packed_1#direct_1,b#packed_1#direct_2,b#packed_1#direct_3,b#packed_1#direct_4;int(1..)])
   ~~> select_cardinality ([("SAT", 20000)])
(a subsetEq b),
and([sum([(a#packed_1 / 1) % 2,(a#packed_1 / 2) % 2;int(1..)]) >= 0,sum([(a#packed_1 / 1) % 2,(a#packed_1 / 2) % 2;int(1..)]) <= 2;int(1..)]),
